========================================================
  ME Attack S1 — Malicious Vehicles, No RSU             
========================================================

  Simulation Parameters:
    Total vehicles        : 20
    Attack percentage     : 40%
    Echo attacker vehicles: 8
    Benign (real link) veh: 20
    Active attack groups  : 4

  Vehicle Roles (NS-3 node IDs):
    V5  [ECHO ATTACKER] <-- in active group
    V6  [ECHO ATTACKER] <-- in active group
    V7  [ECHO ATTACKER] <-- in active group
    V8  [ECHO ATTACKER] <-- in active group
    V9  [ECHO ATTACKER] <-- in active group
    V10  [ECHO ATTACKER] <-- in active group
    V11  [ECHO ATTACKER] <-- in active group
    V12  [ECHO ATTACKER] <-- in active group
    V13  [benign — real link]
    V14  [benign — real link]
    V15  [benign — real link]
    V16  [benign — real link]
    V17  [benign — real link]
    V18  [benign — real link]
    V19  [benign — real link]
    V20  [benign — real link]
    V21  [benign — real link]
    V22  [benign — real link]
    V23  [benign — real link]
    V24  [benign — real link]

  Attack Steps (per group):
  t=10  ①  Real pair HELLO exchange (V2V link); echo pair also exchange own HELLO
  t=10  ②  All four vehicles send legitimate topology to controller
            (no cross-links between real pair and echo pair)
  t=10  ③  Echo vehicles overhear real-pair HELLO (echo preparation)
  t=10+ ④  Echo vehicles send forged echo reports to controller
  t=10+ ⑤  Controller infers phantom paths via echo vehicles
  t=10+ ⑥  Controller installs faulty routes on phantom paths

========================================================

[t=10.001]  STEP ①  NORMAL V2V TOPOLOGY DISCOVERY
  Measured distances: d(V8,V10)=0.601m  d(V6,V10)=4.902m  (range=300.000m)
  V8 -> V10 : HELLO(Sender=V8, t=10.000667)  (real link pair)
  V10 -> V8 : HELLO(Sender=V10, t=10.000667)  (real link pair)
  V6 -> V10 : HELLO(Sender=V6, t=10.000667)  (echo pair — own legitimate link)
  V10 -> V6 : HELLO(Sender=V10, t=10.000667)  (echo pair — own legitimate link)
  [V6 and V10 are in overhearing range of V8↔V10 but have NO direct link to V8 or V10]

[t=10.001]  STEP ②  LEGITIMATE LINK ESTABLISHMENT
  V8 -> Controller : <V8 sees V10, t=10.000667>  (real reporter)
  V10 -> Controller : <V10 sees V8, t=10.000667>  (real reporter)
  V6 -> Controller : <V6 sees V10, t=10.000667>  (echo pair's own real link)
  V10 -> Controller : <V10 sees V6, t=10.000667>  (echo pair's own real link)
  No direct links: V8-V6, V8-V10, V10-V6, V10-V10 do NOT exist

[t=10.001]  STEP ③  HELLO MESSAGE INTERCEPTION (Echo Preparation)
  V6 overhears HELLO: <V8 sees V10, t=10.001>  [stored for echo]
  V10 overhears HELLO: <V8 sees V10, t=10.001>  [stored for echo]
  Echo vehicles will replay this observation to the controller

[t=10.075]  STEP ④  ECHOED TOPOLOGY OBSERVATION REPORTS
  V12 -> Controller : <V8 sees V10, t=10.000667>  (ECHO -- false reporter)
  V9 -> Controller : <V8 sees V10, t=10.000667>  (ECHO -- false reporter)
  Active false reporters: V12, V9
  V3↔V4 link: dist=7.0m  IN RANGE — Path 4 inferred
  V8↔V10 echoed link: dist=0.6m  IN RANGE — genuinely real
  Note: echo vehicles duplicate an existing link — they do NOT fabricate new physical links

[t=10.1]  STEP ⑤  MULTIPATH INFERENCE AT CONTROLLER (Attack Effect)
  Controller aggregates observations for link V8↔V10:
    (1) V8 reports V8↔V10  [real]
    (2) V10 reports V10↔V8  [real]
    (3) V12 reports V8↔V10  [ECHO]
    (4) V9 reports V8↔V10  [ECHO]
  Controller incorrectly infers:
    Path 1: V8 -> V10  (REAL)
    Path 2: V8 -> V12 -> V10  (PHANTOM)
    Path 3: V8 -> V9 -> V10  (PHANTOM)
    Path 4: V8 -> V12 -> V9 -> V10  (PHANTOM — V3↔V4 in range)

[t=10.1]  STEP ⑥  FAULTY ROUTING DECISIONS
  Controller installs routing rules relying on phantom paths
  Packets to V10 may be forwarded via V12 or V9 (including 3-hop V3→V4 chain) (non-existent paths)
  Expected impact: packet loss, increased delay, routing instability
  <- ATTACK SUCCESS
